157: function getDaysRemaining(endDate: string) {
158:   const end = new Date(endDate);
159:   const now = new Date();
160:   const diff = Math.ceil((end.getTime() - now.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
161:   return diff;
162: }

185: function formatDate(d: string) {
186:   return new Date(d).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
187: }
188: 
189: function getDaysRemaining(endDate: string) {
190:   const end = new Date(endDate);
191:   const now = new Date();
192:   return Math.ceil((end.getTime() - now.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
193: }
194:

693:

697:     setSelectedDate(d.toISOString().split('T')[0]);

703:   const elapsed = totalDays - Math.max(0, daysLeft);
704:   const progressPct = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (elapsed / totalDays) * 100));

714:   };

717:     const d = new Date(ds);

720:     if (today.getTime() === target.getTime()) return 'Today';
721:     const yesterday = new Date(today); yesterday.setDate(yesterday.getDate() - 1);
722:     if (yesterday.getTime() === target.getTime()) return 'Yesterday';

690: function StudentProfileDrawer({ student }: { student: any }) {
691:   const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState<string | null>(null);
692:   const [now, setNow] = useState<Date | null>(null);
693: 
694:   useEffect(() => {
695:     const d = new Date('2025-08-09T00:00:00Z');
696:     setNow(d);
697:     setSelectedDate(d.toISOString().split('T')[0]);
698:   }, []);
699: 
700:   if (!student || !now || !selectedDate) return null;
701:   const daysLeft = getDaysRemaining(student.planEnd, now);
702:   const totalDays = Math.ceil((new Date(student.planEnd).getTime() - new Date(student.planStart).getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
703:   const elapsed = totalDays - Math.max(0, daysLeft);
704:   const progressPct = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (elapsed / totalDays) * 100));
705: 
706:   // Get attendance for selected date
707:   const dayLogs = student.attendance?.[selectedDate] || [];
708: 
709:   // Navigate date
710:   const shiftDate = (dir: number) => {
711:     const d = new Date(selectedDate);
712:     d.setDate(d.getDate() + dir);
713:     setSelectedDate(d.toISOString().split('T')[0]);
714:   };
715: 
716:   const formatSelectedDate = (ds: string) => {
717:     const d = new Date(ds);
718:     const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
719:     const target = new Date(ds); target.setHours(0,0,0,0);
720:     if (today.getTime() === target.getTime()) return 'Today';
721:     const yesterday = new Date(today); yesterday.setDate(yesterday.getDate() - 1);
722:     if (yesterday.getTime() === target.getTime()) return 'Yesterday';
723:     return d.toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
724:   };
725:   
726:   return (
727:     <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-[#F8FAFC]">
728:       {/* Drawer Header */}

733:             <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">{student.name}</h2>
734:             <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">{student.id} • {student.phone}</p>
735:             <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-2 flex-wrap">
736:               <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider 
737:                 ${student.status === 'ACTIVE' ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' : 
738:                   student.status === 'EXPIRING' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700' : 
739:                   student.status === 'REVOKED' ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700' :
740:                   'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700'}`}>
741:                 {student.status}
742:               </span>
743:               {student.seat !== 'None' && <span className="text-[11px] bg-slate-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-medium text-slate-600">Seat {student.seat}</span>}
744:               {student.lockerNo && <span className="text-[11px] bg-indigo-50 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-medium text-indigo-600 flex items-center gap-0.5"><Lock className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />{student.lockerNo}</span>}

750: // Sub-component for the Side Drawer Profile
751: function StudentProfileDrawer({ student }: { student: any }) {
752:   const [now, setNow] = useState<Date | null>(null);
753: 
754:   useEffect(() => {
755:     const d = new Date('2025-08-09T00:00:00Z');
756:     setNow(d);
757:   }, []);
758: 
759:   const calendarLogs = useMemo(() => {
760:     const list: { status: string; timestamp: Date }[] = [];
761:     if (student?.attendance) {
762:       for (const [dateStr, events] of Object.entries(student.attendance)) {
763:         for (const ev of events as any[]) {
764:           list.push({
765:             status: ev.type === 'IN' ? 'CHECK_IN' : 'CHECK_OUT',
766:             timestamp: new Date(`${dateStr}T${ev.time}:00`)
767:           });
768:         }
769:       }
770:     }
771:     return list;
772:   }, [student]);
773: 
774:   if (!student || !now) return null;
775:   const daysLeft = getDaysRemaining(student.planEnd, now);
776:   const totalDays = Math.ceil((new Date(student.planEnd).getTime() - new Date(student.planStart).getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
777:   const elapsed = totalDays - Math.max(0, daysLeft);
778:   const progressPct = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (elapsed / totalDays) * 100));
779:   
780:   return (
781:     <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-[#F8FAFC]">
782:       {/* Drawer Header */}
783:       <div className="p-6 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 shrink-0">
784:         <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
785:           <img src={student.avatar} alt="" className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl object-cover shadow-sm ring-1 ring-slate-200" />
786:           <div className="flex-1">
787:             <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 leading-tight">{student.name}</h2>
788:             <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">{student.id} • {student.phone}</p>
789:             <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-2 flex-wrap">
790:               <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider 
791:                 ${student.status === 'ACTIVE' ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' : 
792:                   student.status === 'EXPIRING' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700' : 
793:                   student.status === 'REVOKED' ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700' :
794:                   'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700'}`}>
795:                 {student.status}
796:               </span>
797:               {student.seat !== 'None' && <span className="text-[10px] bg-slate-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-medium text-slate-600">Seat {student.seat}</span>}
798:               {student.lockerNo && <span className="text-[10px] bg-indigo-50 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-medium text-indigo-600 flex items-center gap-1"><Lock className="w-3 h-3" />{student.lockerNo}</span>}
799:             </div>
800:           </div>
801:         </div>
802:       </div>
803: 
804:       {/* Drawer Content */}
805:       <div className="flex-1 p-6 overflow-y-auto">
806:         <div className="space-y-6">
807: 
808:           {/* Current Plan with progress */}
809:           <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
810:             <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
811:               <div>
812:                 <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Current Plan</div>
813:                 <div className="font-bold text-xl text-slate-900 mt-1">{student.plan}</div>
814:                 <div className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">{formatDate(student.planStart)} → {formatDate(student.planEnd)}</div>
815:               </div>
816:               {(student.status === 'ACTIVE' || student.status === 'EXPIRING') && daysLeft > 0 ? (
817:                 <div className={`text-base font-bold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg ${daysLeft <= 7 ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 border border-amber-100' : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 border border-emerald-100'}`}>
818:                   {daysLeft}d left
819:                 </div>
820:               ) : student.status === 'REVOKED' ? (
821:                 <div className="text-base font-bold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 border border-rose-100">Revoked</div>
822:               ) : (
823:                 <div className="text-base font-bold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-slate-500 border border-slate-100">Expired</div>
824:               )}
825:             </div>
826:             {/* Progress bar */}
827:             {(student.status === 'ACTIVE' || student.status === 'EXPIRING') && (

834:                   <div className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all ${progressPct > 80 ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-indigo-500'}`} style={{ width: `${progressPct}%` }} />

836:               </div>
